[[Bahrain]].\n{{TOC limit|2}}\n\n==Brave CF Flyweight Tournament==\n=== Background ===\nBrave Combat Federation will look to crown its first Flyweight world champion with an 8-man tournament.\n\nThe eight participants of the tournament were revealed. They included the former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bantamweight World Champion]] [[Zach Makovsky]], the formers UFC fighter [[Dustin Ortiz]], [[Ali Bagautinov]], Jose Torres as well as [[Abdul Hussein (fighter)|Abdul Hussein]], Flavio de Queiroz, Velimurad Alkhasov and the former [[Cage Fury Fighting Championships#CFFC Flyweight Championship|Cage Fury Flyweight Champion]] Sean Santella.\n\n=== BRAVE CF Flyweight Tournament bracket ===\n{{8TeamBracket|seeds=n|nowrap=y\n| RD1=Quarter-finals|RD2=Semi-finals| RD3=Final\n\n| RD1-team1={{flagicon|BRA}} 10==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Brave CF 46: Krepost Selection 10\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]

[[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] in partnership with Krepost FC on January 16, 2021, at the WOW Arena in [[Sochi]], [[Russia]].\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a Super Lightweight title bout between the reigning Brave Super Lightweight champion [[Eldar Eldarov]] and the former [[UFC]] lightweight [[Leonardo Mafra]].{{cite web |last1=LockerRoom Team |title=Road to Brave CF 46: Eldar Eldarov and Abdulmanap Nurmagomedov, Rise of Mafra |url=https://lockerroom.in/blog/view/Eldar-Eldarov-Abdulmanap-Nurmagomedov-Mafra |website=lockerroom.in |access-date=8 March 2021}}\n\nIn the co-main event, the one-time [[List of UFC Champions#Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight title]] challenger [[Ali Bagautinov]] was scheduled to fight Oleg Lichkovakha in a 59.5 kg catchweight bout.{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Brave CF 46: Returning Ali Bagautinov 'hungry and angry' ahead of Brave CF debut [[Bahrain]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''Brave CF 47: Asian Domination''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on March 11, 2021, at the [[Arad Fort]] in [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]].\n\n=== Background ===\nA lightweight title eliminator between [[Rolando Dy]] and Abdysalam Uulu Kubanychiev was scheduled as the main event of the card.{{cite web |last1=Leongson |first1=Randolph B. |title=Rolando Dy inches closer to title shot, faces tough Kyrgyz foe |url=https://www.spin.ph/mma/rolando-dy-inches-closer-to-title-shot-faces-tough-kyrgyz-foe-a2437-20210303 |website=spin.ph |access-date=8 March 2021}}\n\nTwo undefeated featherweight prospects were scheduled to fight in the co-main event: Tae Kyun Kim and Husein Kadimagomaev.{{cite news |title=Undefeated young stars Husein Kadimagomaev, Tae Kyun Kim to clash at Brave CF 47 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/undefeated-young-stars-husein-kadimagomaev-tae-kyun-kim-to-clash-at-brave-cf-47/ |access-date=28 January 2022 |work=Fightbook MMA |date=18 February 2021}}\n\n[[Nursulton Ruziboev]] was set to face off against Andreas Stahl, but the Swede was forced to drop out due to injury. Ibrahim Mane will step up on five days\u2019 notice to take on Ruziboev.{{cite news |last1=Mcdade |first1=Leo |title=Ibrahim Mane steps up in place of Stahl, takes on Ruziboev at Brave CF 47 event\n|name=Brave CF 48: Arabian Night\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]\n|image=\n|date=March 18, 2021\n|venue=[[Arad Fort]]\n|city={{flagicon|BHR}} [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''Brave CF 48: Arabian Night''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on March 18, 2021, at the [[Arad Fort]] in [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]].\n\n=== Background ===\nThe event was headlined by a lightweight bout between two promotional newcomers, the PFL veterant Ylies Djiroun has met the former ACA fighter Abdul-Rakhman Makhazhiev.{{cite web |last1=Clifton |first1=Michael |title=Brave CF 48 Full Lineup Announced |url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/brave-cf-48-full-lineup-announced/ |website=lowkickmma.com |access-date=8 March 2021}}\n\nTariq Ismail and Nkosi Ndebele were scheduled to fight at bantamweight, but Ismail was forced off the card due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite news |title=Top Bantamweights Tariq Ismail and Nkosi Ndebele to clash at Brave CF 48: Arabian Nights |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/top-bantamweights-tariq-ismail-and-nkosi-ndebele-to-clash-at-brave-cf-48-arabian-nights/ |access-date=28 January 2022 |work=Fightbook MMA |date=25 February 2021}} Ndebele instead faced Abdelmoumen Mssaate, who stepped in on short notice for this encounter.{{cite web |title=Nkosi Ndebele will continue his rise towards stardom when he meets Abdelmoumen Mssaate 48|url=https://www.tapology.com/fightcenter/events/77282-brave-cf-48-arabian-night|access-date=2020-08-05|website=Tapology|language=en}}\n\n== Brave CF 49: Super Fights ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Brave CF 49: Super Fights\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]\n|image=\n|image_size=\n|date=March 25, 2021\n|venue=[[Arad Fort]]\n|city={{flagicon|BHR}} [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''Brave CF 49: Super Fights''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event scheduled to be held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on March 25, 2021, at the [[Arad Fort]] in [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]].\n\n=== Background ===\nThe event was headlined by a fight between Luan Santiago and [[Beno\u00eet Saint-Denis]] in a super lightweight bout.{{cite news |title=Former world champion Luan Santiago to take on undefeated Benoit St. Denis at Brave CF 49 |url=https://mmauk.net/2021/03/03/former-world-champion-luan-santiago-to-take-on-undefeated-benoit-st-denis-at-brave-cf-49/ |access-date=28 January 2022 |work=MMA UK |date=3 March 2021}}\n\nThe co-main event featured a rematch between [[Jose Torres (fighter)|Jose Torres]] and Sean Santella. The fight is part of the Brave Flyweight Tournament, with their first fight ending in a draw.{{cite web |last1=LockerRoom Team |title=Brave CF 49: Jose Torres vs. Sean Santella 2 to serve as the main event |url=https://lockerroom.in/blog/view/BRAVE-CF-49-Jose-Torres-Sean-Santella |website=lockerroom.in |access-date=8 March 2021}} The week of the event, Santella had to pull out due to injury and was replaced by SBG Ireland's Blaine O\u2019Driscoll. While the fight will be contested at a Catchweight of 61 kg, due to the short-notice nature of the match-up, the bout between Torres and O\u2019Driscoll will indeed serve as a quarter-final fight for the Flyweight tournament.{{cite web |title=Sean Santella injured, Shorty Torres now faces Blaine O\u2019Driscoll in Flyweight tourney at Brave CF 49 |url=https://mymmanews.com/sean-santella-injured-shorty-torres-now-faces-blaine-odriscoll-in-flyweight-tourney-at-brave-cf-49/ |website=MyMMANews.com |access-date=22 March 2021 |date=20 March 2021}}\n\nTwo [[International Mixed Martial Arts Federation|IMMAF]] flyweight standouts, [[Muhammad Mokaev]] and Abdul Hussein, were scheduled to fight at the event.{{cite web |title=Brave CF 49: Muhammad Mokaev takes on fellow amateur world champion Abdul Hussein in Flyweight debut |url=http://mmauk.net/2021/03/03/brave-cf-49-muhammad-mokaev-takes-on-fellow-amateur-world-champion-abdul-hussein-in-flyweight-debut/ |website=MMA UK |access-date=8 March 2021}}\n\n===Results===\n{| 49|url=https://www.tapology.com/fightcenter/events/77283-brave-cf-49-super-fights|access-date=2020-08-05|website=Tapology|language=en}}\n\n== Brave CF 50 ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Brave CF 50\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]\n|image=\n|date=April 1, 2021\n|venue=[[Arad Fort]]\n|city={{flagicon|BHR}} [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''Brave CF 50''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on April 1, 2021, at the [[Arad Fort]] in [[Arad, Bahrain|Arad]], [[Bahrain]].\n\n=== Background ===\nThis event featured a title fight between the champion Jarrah Hussein Al-Silawi and the promotional newcomer [[Ismail Naurdiev]] for the [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Welterweight Championship|Brave Welterweight title]] as the event headliner.{{cite web |last1=Hannoun |first1=Farah |title=Brave CF 50: Super welterweight champion Jarrah Al-Silawi set for Ismail Naurdiev test on April 1 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/brave-cf-50-super-welterweight-champion-jarrah-al-silawi-set-for-ismail-naurdiev-test-on-april-1 |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |access-date=8 March 2021}}\n\nThe co-main event was set to feature a fight for the vacant [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Light Heavyweight Championship|Brave Light Heavyweight title]], between the current Brave Middleweight champion Mohammad Fakhreddine and Mohamed Said Maalem.{{cite news |last1=Anderson |first1=Frankie |title=Fakhreddine aims double-champ status against Mohammed Said Maalem at Brave CF 50 |url=https://mmauk.net/2021/03/04/fakhreddine-aims-double-champ-status-against-mohammed-said-maalem-at-brave-cf-50/ |access-date=28 January 2022 |work=MMA UK |date=4 March 2021}} However, on the day of the event, Fakhreddine had to pull out due to illness, the bout was postponed.\n\nAmin Ayoub was scheduled to make the first defense of his [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Lightweight Championship|Brave Lightweight title]], against the 8-fight Brave veteran Ahmed Amir.{{cite web |title=Amin Ayoub to defend Lightweight belt against Ahmed Amir at Brave CF 50 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/amin-ayoub-to-defend-lightweight-belt-against-ahmed-amir-at-brave-cf-50/ |website=Fightbook MMA |access-date=8 March 2021}} However, a week before the event, Amir had to pull out due to injury and was replaced by Mashrabjon Ruziboev, who would fight Ayoub in a 74 kg catchweight non-title fight.{{cite web |title=Amin Ayoub - Changement de programme suite au forfait d'Ahmed Amir |url=https://boxemag.com/actualites/item/amin-ayoub-changement-de-programme-suite-au-forfait-d-ahmed-amir.html |website=boxemag.com |access-date=11 July 2021}}\n\nThe Brave flyweight tournament has continued with a semi-finals bout who has featured a rematch between Velimurad Alkhasov and [[Zach Makovsky]],{{cite web |last1=Staff Report |title=Zach Makovsky vs Velimurad Alkhasov rematch set for Brave CF Flyweight tournament |url=https://mymmanews.com/zach-makovsky-vs-velimurad-alkhasov-rematch-set-for-brave-cf-flyweight-tournament/ |website=mymmanews.com |access-date=8 March 2021}} and a quarter-final bout between two formers [[UFC]] flyweights, [[Ali Bagautinov]] and [[Dustin Ortiz]].{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Ali Bagautinov joins Brave CF's flyweight tournament, targets Dustin Ortiz showdown |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/01/brave-cf-ali-bagautinov-joins-flyweight-tournament-targets-dustin-ortiz |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |access-date=8 March 2021}}\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | BRAVE CF 50|url=https://www.tapology.com/fightcenter/events/77284-brave-cf-50|access-date=2020-08-05|website=Tapology|language=en}}\n\n== Brave CF 51: The Future Is Here ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Brave CF 51: The Future Is Here\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]\n|image=\n|date=June 04, 2021\n|venue=Falcon Club\n|city={{flagicon|BLR}} [[Minsk]], Belarus\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''Brave CF 51: The Future Is Here''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on June 4, 2021, at the Falcon Club in [[Minsk]]. Belarus will become the 21st country to host a Brave CF show.{{Cite web|last=Bahrain|first=mixed martial arts-|title=Brave CF 51 To Make Belarus Debut\n|url=https://www.overtimeheroics.net/2021/04/12/brave-cf-51-to-make-belarus-debut/|website=overtimeheroics.net|language=en}}\n\n=== Background ===\nFormer [[UFC]] fighter [[Lucas Martins]] was scheduled to fight Marcel Grabinski in the main event.{{cite web |last1=Petzel-Gligorea |first1=Alexander |title=Brave CF 51: Marcel Mohamed Grabinski im Hauptkampf gegen UFC-Veteran Lucas Martins |url=https://www.gnp1.de/mma/mma-deutschland/brave-cf-51-marcel-mohamed-grabinski-im-hauptkampf-gegen-ufc-veteran-lucas-martins |website=gnp1.de |access-date=30 April 2021}} However, Grabinski has to pull out from the fight due to medical condition. The bout has been called off.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=Mager Smashes Sagyntay Atop Brave CF 51 After Martins-Grabinski Canceled|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Mager-Smashes-Sagyntay-Atop-Brave-CF-51-After-MartinsGrabinski-Canceled-179758|access-date=2021-06-05|website=Sherdog}}\n\nIn the co-main event Denis Maher was scheduled to fight Giannis Bachar in a welterweight bout, but Bachar has been forced to withdraw due to an injury.{{cite web |last1=LockerRoom Team |title=Brave CF 51: Undefeated Denis Maher to fight Giannis Bachar in the co-main event |url=https://lockerroom.in/blog/view/BRAVE-CF-51-Denis-Maher-Giannis-Bachar |website=lockerroom.in |access-date=30 April 2021}} Fernando Gonzalez has stepped in as a replacement.{{cite web |last1=FightBook MMA |title=Belarus star Denis Maher draws new opponent, takes on Fernando Gonzalez at Brave CF 51 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/belarus-star-denis-maher-draws-new-opponent-takes-on-fernando-gonzalez-at-brave-cf-51/|website=fightbookmma.com |access-date=3 May 2021}} Unfortunately, Gonzalez has been forced to pull due to an undisclosed injury. Maher instead faced Rinat Sagyntay, who stepped in on 3 weeks notice for this encounter.{{cite web |title=Denis Maher gets new opponent, meets Rinat Sagyntay in new Brave CF 51 co-main event |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/denis-maher-gets-new-opponent-meets-rinat-sagyntay-in-new-brave-cf-51-co-main-event/ |website=fightbookmma.com |access-date=17 May 2021}}\n\nFrenchmen Ylies Djiroun fought the brit Sam Patterson in lightweight bout.{{cite web |title=Ylies Djiroun returns against Sam Patterson in pivotal lightweight fight at Brave CF 51 |url=https://www.explica.co/ylies-djiroun-returns-against-sam-patterson-in-pivotal-lightweight-fight-at-brave-cf-51.html |website=explica.co |access-date=30 April 2021 |archive-date=30 April 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210430045457/https://www.explica.co/ylies-djiroun-returns-against-sam-patterson-in-pivotal-lightweight-fight-at-brave-cf-51.html |url-status=dead }} One-time [[UFC]] fighter Roman Bogatov fought Abdulmutalip Gairbekov in a featherweight bout.{{cite web |title=Roman Bogatov takes on Abdulmutalip Gairbekov in all-Russian Featherweight clash at Brave CF 51 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/roman-bogatov-takes-on-abdulmutalip-gairbekov-in-all-russian-featherweight-clash-at-brave-cf-51/ |website=Fightbook MMA |access-date=30 April 51|url=https://www.tapology.com/fightcenter/events/78536-brave-cf-51|access-date=2020-08-05|website=Tapology|language=en}}\n\n== Brave CF 52: Bad Blood==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Brave CF 52: Bad Blood\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]\n|image=\n|date=August 1, 2021\n|venue=\n|city={{flagicon|ITA}} [[Milan]], [[Italy]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''Brave CF 52: Bad Blood''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] in partnership with The Golden Cage promotion on August 1, 2021, in [[Milan]], [[Italy]]. Italy will become the 22nd country to host a Brave CF show.\n\n===Background===\nBrave middleweight champion Mohammad Fakhreddine faced Mohamed Said Maalem for the vacant BRAVE Light Heavyweight title. The two of them were scheduled to fight at [[#BRAVE CF 50|Brave CF 50]], before Fakhreddine withdrew due to illness.{{cite web |last1=Duncan |first1=Steven |title=Brave CF 52: el campe\u00f3n de peso mediano Fakhreddine se enfrenta a su archirrival Said Maalem por el t\u00edtulo vacante de peso semipesado |url=https://mma.uno/brave-cf-52-el-campeon-de-peso-mediano-fakhreddine-se-enfrenta-a-su-archirrival-said-maalem-por-el-titulo-vacante-de-peso-semipesado/ |website=mma.uno |access-date=11 July 2021}} Mohamed Said Maalem was strip of the title by FIGMMA for intentional punches opponent's back of the head.{{cite web |last1=Leonte |first1=Tudor |title=FIGMMA Overturns Mohamed Said Maalem's Victory in Brave CF 52 Headliner |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/FIGMMA-Overturns-Mohamed-Said-Maalems-Victory-in-Brave-CF-52-Headliner-181305 |website=sherdog.com |access-date=27 August 2021}}\n\nTwo additions to the card were announced on July 1: A middleweight bout between Enrico Cortese and the 2015 ADCC bronze medalist Rustam Chsiev, as well as a featherweight bout between the former featherweight champion Elias Boudegzdame and the promotional newcomer Declan Dalton.{{cite web |last1=LockerRoom Team |title=Brave CF 52: Complete fight card revealed for the event in Italy |url=https://lockerroom.in/blog/view/BRAVE-CF-52-Fight-Card |website=lockerroom.in |access-date=11 July 2021}}\n\nThe five-fight Brave veteran Gamzat Magomedov is scheduled to meet the undefeated Mochamed Machaev in a bantamweight bout.{{cite web | title=Brave CF 52: Mochamed Machaev to take on Gamzat Magomedov in pivotal Bantamweight bout Santalahti was scheduled to fight the three-fight Brave veteran Kevin Ruart in a super-welterweight bout.{{cite web |last1=Nurminen |first1=Jyrki |title=Olli Santalahti vs Kevin Ruart: \"Tavoitteeni complements the undercard for Brave CF's debut in Italy |url=https://www.explica.co/abdoul-vs-nursulton-complements-the-undercard-for-brave-cfs-debut-in-italy.html |website=explica.co |access-date=11 Martins]] and Marcel Grabinski was announced for the event. The two were originally scheduled to fight at Brave CF 51, before Grabinski withdrew from the bout due to health issues.{{cite web |title=Lucas Martins vs Marcel Grabinski rescheduled for Brave CF 53 in Kazakhstan |url=https://www.explica.co/lucas-martins-vs-marcel-grabinski-rescheduled-for-brave-cf-53-in-kazakhstan.html |website=explica.co |access-date=23 July 2021 |archive-date=23 July 2021 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210723134346/https://www.explica.co/lucas-martins-vs-marcel-grabinski-rescheduled-for-brave-cf-53-in-kazakhstan.html |url-status=dead }} According to Brave CF officials, Grabinski was forced to withdraw from the rescheduled bout a little bit less than week prior to the event due to COVID-19 protocols. League of Serbia on December 18, 2021, in [[Belgrade]], [[Serbia]]. Serbia will become the 24th country to host a Brave CF show.{{cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/brave-cf-will-become-the-first-global-promotion-to-host-event-in-serbia/|title=Brave CF Will Become The First Global Promotion To Host Event In Serbia|website=FightBook MMA|date=2021-11-17|access-date=2021-11-21}}\n\n===Background===\nA middleweight bout between Chad Hanekom and Miro Jurkovi\u0107 was slated to serve as the event co-headliner.{{cite web |last1=Duncan |first1=Steve |title=Chad Hanekom regresa contra el h\u00e9roe local Miro Jurkovic en el Brave CF 56 en Serbia |url=https://mma.uno/chad-hanekom-regresa-contra-el-heroe-local-miro-jurkovic-en-el-brave-cf-56-en-serbia/ |website=mma.uno |access-date=22 November 2021}} However, on December 12, Hanekom have to withdraw for this event due to travel restrictions. Mikhail Allakhverdian step in on short notice to face Jurkovi\u0107 in the co-main event.{{cite web |title=Allakhverdian steps in for Hanekom, faces Jurkovic in co-main event of Brave CF 56 Maritim Hotel\n| {{flagicon|GER}} [[D\u00fcsseldorf]], [[Germany]]\n|}\n\n==BRAVE CF Flyweight Tournament==\n=== Background ===\nBRAVE Combat Federation will look to crown its first Flyweight world champion with a 8-man tournament.\n\nThe eight participants of the tournament were revealed. They included the former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Bantamweight World Championship|Bantamweight World Champion]] [[Zach Makovsky]], the formers UFC fighter [[Dustin Ortiz]], [[Ali Bagautinov]], [[Jose Torres (fighter)|Jose Torres]] as well as Abdul Hussein, Flavio de Queiroz, Velimurad Alkhasov and the former [[Cage Fury Fighting Championships#CFFC Flyweight Championship|Cage Fury Flyweight Champion]] Sean Santella.\n\n=== BRAVE CF Flyweight Tournament bracket ===\n{{8TeamBracket-NoSeeds | RD1=Quarter-finals|RD2=Semi-finals| RD3=Final\n\n| RD1-team1={{flagicon|BRA}} Flavio de Queiroz\n| \n|align=center|2\n|align=center|4:50\n|\n|}\n\n== BRAVE CF 57 ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=BRAVE CF 57 \n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]\n|image=\n|date=March 11, 2022\n|venue=[[Khalifa Sports City Stadium]]\n|city={{flagicon|BHR}} [[Isa Town]], [[Bahrain]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''BRAVE CF 57''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on March 11, 2022, at the [[Khalifa Sports City Stadium]] in [[Isa Town]], [[Bahrain]].\n\n=== Background ===\nA [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Bantamweight Championship|BRAVE CF Bantamweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between Hamza Kooheji and [[Brad Katona]] was scheduled as the event headliner.{{cite web |last1=Pimentel |first1=Anatoly |title=BRAVE 57: Hamza Kooheji and Brad Katona Showdown for Bantamweight Title |url=https://www.overtimeheroics.net/2022/01/13/brave-57-hamza-kooheji-and-brad-katona-showdown-for-bantamweight-title/ |website=overtimeheroics.net |access-date=5 February 2022 |date=13 January 2022}}\n\nTwo additional title fights were scheduled for the event: a [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Light Heavyweight Championship|BRAVE CF Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between Mohamed Said Maalem and Mohammad Fakhreddine,{{cite web |last1=FightBookMMA |title=Fakhreddine Vs. Said Maalem II For Light Heavyweight World Title Booked For BRAVE CF 57 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/fakhreddine-vs-said-maalem-ii-for-light-heavyweight-world-title-booked-for-brave-cf-57/ |website=fightbookmma.com |access-date=5 February 2022}} as well as an interim [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Lightweight Championship|BRAVE CF Lightweight Championship]] bout between Abdisalam Kubanychbek and Amin Ayoub.{{cite web |last1=FightBookMMA |title=Champ Amir Out Indefinitely, Ayoub To Face Kubanychbek For Interim Title At BRAVE CF 57 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/champ-amir-out-indefinitely-ayoub-to-face-kubanychbek-for-interim-title-at-brave-cf-57/ |website=fightbookmma.com |access-date=5 February 2022 |date=4 February 2022}}\n\nA flyweight bout between Rizvan Abuev and [[Asu Almabayev]] was planned for the event. However, Abuev was forced to pull out due to broken ankle. Almabayev takes on Imram Magaramov who step in on 2 weeks notice for the fight.{{cite web |title=March 11 in Bahrain at the Brave 57 tournament |url=https://twitter.com/MMACENTRALASIA/status/1495815826036957186 |website=MMA CENTRAL ASIA |access-date=22 February 2022}}\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! Beast Championship Federation\n|image=\n|date=April 30, 2022\n|venue=[[Samsan World Gymnasium]]\n|city={{flagicon|KOR}} [[Incheon]], [[South Korea]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''BRAVE CF 58''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] in partnership with Beast Championship Federation on April 30, 2022, at the [[Samsan World Gymnasium]] in [[Incheon]], [[South Korea]]. South Korea will become the 26th country to host a BRAVE CF show.\n\n=== Background ===\nThe main event was set to feature a featherweight bout between Tae Kyun Kim and Roman Bogatov,{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Brave CF 58: Tae Kyun Kim set to star on home soil in promotion\u2019s first South Korean show |url=https://www.fightersonlymag.com/latest-news/brave-cf-58-tae-kyun-kim-set-to-star-on-home-soil-in-promotions-first-south-korean-show/ |website=fightersonlymag.com |access-date=23 April 2022}} but Kim has been forced to pull out due to a hand injury and the bout with Bogatov has been scratched. A Middleweight bout between Mzwandile Hlongwa and In Jae La, which has been previously announced for the card, will now serve as the main event.{{cite web |title=Injured Kim ruled out of BRAVE CF 58 main event |url=https://www.gdnonline.com/Details/1049859/Injured-Kim-ruled-out-of-BRAVE-CF-58-main-event |website=gdnonline.com |access-date=23 April 2022}}\n\nA super lightweight bout between Maciej Gierszewski and Mihail Kotru\u0163\u0103 was planned for the event. However, Kotru\u0163\u0103 was out due to injury and Issa Isakov was in against Gierszewski.{{cite web |title=Mihail Kotruta out, Issa Isakov steps in to take on \"Magic Man\" at BRAVE CF 58 |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/mihail-kotruta-out-issa-isakov-steps-in-to-take-on-magic-man-at-brave-cf-58/ |website=fightbookmma.com |access-date=23 April 2022}}\n\n===Results===\n{| ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=BRAVE CF 59\n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]
Amir Temur Fighting Championship \n|image=\n|date=June 18, 2022\n|venue=Amphitheater Bukhara\n|city={{flagicon|UZB}} [[Bukhara]], [[Uzbekistan]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''BRAVE CF 59''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] in partnership with Amir Temur Fighting Championship on June 18, 2022, in [[Bukhara]], [[Uzbekistan]]. Uzbekistan will become the 27th country to host a BRAVE CF show.\n\n=== Background ===\nAsuper lightweight bout between Baz Mohammad and Ayub Gazievwas planned for the event. However, on July 24, due to unforeseen circumstances the event was moved from Brazil to the Kingdom of Bahrain.\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: #000080; text-align: center;\" | '''BRAVE CF 60'''\n|-\n! [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on October 19, 2022, at the [[Khalifa Sports City Stadium]] in [[Isa Town]], [[Bahrain]].\n\n=== Background ===\nThis event featured two title fights, first a super welterweight bout between [[Marcin Bandel]] and Ismail Naurdiev for the vacant BRAVE Combat Federation Super Welterweight Championship as event headliner and in the co-main event a bantamweight bout the champion Brad Katona and Gamzat Magomedov for the BRAVE Combat Federation Bantamweight Championship.{{cite web |title=Naurdiev to face Bandel for welterweight Federation]]\n|image=\n|date=February 18, 2023\n|venue=[[Stark Arena]]\n|city={{flagicon|SRB}} [[Belgrade]], [[Serbia]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''BRAVE CF 69''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on February 18, 2023 at the [[Stark Arena]] in [[Belgrade]], [[Serbia]].\n\n=== Background ===\nAbdisalam Kubanychbek of Kyrgyzstan, the interim lightweight champion of BRAVE CF, will face Kamil Magomedov of Russia in the main event for the chance to unify the 155-pound global championship. This situation developed after Ahmed Amir, a previous lineal champion, had his title removed because of doubts about his ability to compete again.{{cite web |title=Fight Site - Brave CF objavio cijeli \u2018card\u2018 za priredbu u Beogradu: Nastupa \u0161est regionalnih boraca |url=https://fightsite.hr/fightsite/regija/brave-cf-objavio-cijeli-card-za-priredbu-u-beogradu-nastupa-sest-regionalnih-boraca-15303166 |website=fightsite.hr |access-date=15 February 2023 |language=hr-hr |date=6 February 2023}}{{cite web |last1= |first1=|title=Other Sports: Gaziev prepares for career-changing fight |url=https://www.gdnonline.com/Details/1174186 |website=www.gdnonline.com |access-date=15 February 2023}}\n\nAleksandar \"Joker\" Ilic, a local celebrity, competes against Marcin Naruszczka of Poland in the third and final fight of the evening in a 90-kilogram catchweight match. When the bout between \"Joker\" and Brazil's Cleber \"Clebinho\" Sousa was cancelled, Naruszczka was given the chance to step in and shock the world and achieve the biggest victory of his career - in front of a crowd that hadn't seen their hero fight live since 2018.\n\n===Results===\n{| class=\"wikitable\" style=\"font-size: 80%;\"\n|-\n! colspan=\"8\" style=\"background-color: #ccf; color: \n|promotion=[[Brave Combat Federation]]
Victorious Fighting Entertainment\n|image=\n|date=December 7, 2023\n|venue=Centro de Forma\u00e7\u00e3o Ol\u00edmpica\n|city={{flagicon|BRA}} [[Fortaleza]], [[Brazil]]\n|attendance=\n}}\n\n'''BRAVE CF 78: Victorious''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Brave Combat Federation]] on December 7, 2023 at the Centro de Forma\u00e7\u00e3o Ol\u00edmpica in [[Fortaleza]], [[Brazil]].\n\n=== Background ===\n\nAn inaugural [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Flyweight Championship|BRAVE CF Flyweight Championship]] bout between Velimurad Alkhasov and Edilceu Alves was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/russia-vs-brazilin-brazil-brave-cf-78-headlined-by-inaugural-flyweight-world-title-fight/|title=Russia vs Brazil\u2026in Brazil! BRAVE CF 78 headlined by inaugural Flyweight world title fight|website=FightBook MMA|access-date=2023-10-26}} However, due to undisclosed reasons, Alkhasov has pulled out of the title fight and will be replaced by Flavio de Queiroz.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/brave-cf-78-gets-new-main-event-as-flavio-queiroz-steps-in-to-fight-for-the-inaugural-flyweight-world-titl/|title=BRAVE CF 78 gets new main event, as Flavio Queiroz steps in to fight for the inaugural Flyweight world title|website=FightBook MMA|access-date=2023-11-12}} Ten days later, it was Flavio's turn to withdraw after the latter fell ill. As a results, a super lightweight bout between Joilton Lutterbach and former [[Brave Combat Federation#BRAVE Combat Federation Lightweight Championship|BRAVE CF Lightweight]] champion Luan Santiago was promoted to the main event status.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/and-the-curse-continues-flavio-out-of-brave-cf-78-lutterbach-vs-santiago-moved-to-main-event-full-card-announced/|title=And the curse continues! Flavio out of BRAVE CF 78, Lutterbach vs Santiago moved to main event \u2013 full card announced|website=FightBook MMA|access-date=2023-11-22}} At the weigh-in, Lutterbach missed the weight limit and was removed from the card. In the end, a super welterweight bout between Luiz Cado and Vernon Ramos was decided to lead the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/brave-cf-78-crazy-scenes-as-weight-misses-shift-entire-fight-card-with-new-main-event-on-deck/|title=BRAVE CF 78: crazy scenes as weight misses shift entire fight Czech Republic.\n\n===Background===\nA [[Oktagon MMA#Featherweight championship|Oktagon Featherweight Championship]] for the vacant title between former [[Oktagon MMA#Lightweight championship|Oktagon Lightweight Champion]] [[Losene Keita]] and Niko Samsonidse headlined the event. Former champion Mate Sanikidze announced that he vacated the title when he moved down to bantamweight.{{Cite web |date=2023-10-17 |title=Losene Keita returns to challenge for featherweight title at Oktagon 50 \n|url=https://fightersonly.com/article/ext/83942 |access-date=2025-04-20 |website=Fighters Only |language=en-US}}\n\nA Oktagon Lightweight Championship rematch for the vacant title between Ronald Paradeiser and former champion (also former Oktagon Featherweight Champion) Ivan Buchinger served as co-main event. The pairing previously met at Oktagon 27 in February 2021, with Buchinger captured the title via first round submission.{{Cite web |title=Oktagon 50: Lightweight title rematch added as Paradeiser and Buchinger run it back in Ostrava \n|url=https://www.fightersonlymag.com/latest-news/oktagon-50-lightweight-title-rematch-added-as-paradeiser-and-buchinger-run-it-back-in-ostrava/ |access-date=2025-04-20 |website=Fighters Only |language=en-US}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (DAZN)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight \n|[[Losene Keita]]\n|def.\n|Niko event\n|name=Oktagon 66: Engizek vs. Oniszczuk\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Oktagon MMA]]\n|date=February 1, 2025\n|venue=[[PSD Bank Dome]]\n|city=[[D\u00fcsseldorf]], Germany \n|attendance=\n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n\n'''''Oktagon 66: Engizek vs. Oniszczuk''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Oktagon MMA]] on February 1, 2025, in [[D\u00fcsseldorf]], Germany.\n\n===Background===\nThe first round of [[#Oktagon Middleweight Tournament|Oktagon Middleweight Tournament]] between the current [[Oktagon MMA#Middleweight championship|Oktagon Middleweight Champion]] Kerim Engizek and Anthony Salamone was expected to headline the event. While, the event took place the first round middleweight tournament between Mat\u011bj Pe\u0148\u00e1z and [[David Zawada]].{{Cite web |last=Staff |date=2024-12-28 |title=Oktagon MMA Announces Match-Ups for \u20ac1 Million Middleweight Tournament \n|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/12/28/oktagon-mma-announces-match-ups-for-e1-million-middleweight-tournament/ |access-date=2025-01-01 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\nHowever, Salamone and Damien Lapilus were removed from the event due to two fighters respective doping violations and subsequent suspensions by the [[French Anti-Doping Agency]].{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-01-30 |title=Sakra! Francouzsk\u00e1 komise doslova hodila vidle do turnaje OKTAGON 66 \n|url=https://mmashorties.cz/sakra-francouzska-komise-doslova-hodila-vidle-do-turnaje-oktagon-66/ |access-date=2025-01-31 |website=MMA Shorties |language=cs}} As a results, Lapilus opponent Lom-Ali Eskiev faced Wanderley Junior instead.{{Cite web |date=2025-01-30 |title=Lapilus pre ban za doping nenast\u00fapi. Toto je n\u00e1hradn\u00fd s\u00faper pre Nemca Lom-Ali Eskieva na OKTAGON 66 \n|url=https://www.mmafanatik.eu/lapilus-pre-ban-za-doping-nenastupi-toto-je-nahradny-super-pre-nemca-lom-ali-eskieva-na-oktagon-66/ |access-date=2025-01-31 |website=MMA Fanatik |language=sk}} Engizek faced [[Kamil Oniszczuk]], who was scheduled to face Hojat Khajevand at this event.{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-01-31 |title=Tak zachr\u00e1n\u011bno! \u0160ampion Kerim Engizek m\u00e1 nov\u00e9ho soupe\u0159e \n|url=https://mmashorties.cz/tak-zachraneno-sampion-kerim-engizek-ma-noveho-soupere/ |access-date=2025-01-31 |website=MMA Shorties |language=cs}} Khajevand completed at this event against Kennedy Rayomba instead.{{Cite web |last=McCorry |first=Patrick |date=2025-01-31 |title=Oktagon 66 Suffers Major Blow Due To Doping Violations \n|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/01/31/oktagon-66-suffers-major-blow-due-to-doping-violations/ |access-date=2025-02-01 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n===Bonus awards===\nThe following fighters received \u20ac5,000 bonus.\n*'''Performance of the Night: Deniz Ilbay'''\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Oktagon.tv)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight Oktagon 72: V\u00e9mola vs. V\u00e9gh 3 ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Oktagon 72: V\u00e9mola vs. V\u00e9gh 3\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Oktagon MMA]]\n|date=June 14, 2025\n|venue=[[Fortuna Arena]]\n|city=[[Prague]], Czech Republic \n|attendance=\n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n\n'''''Oktagon 72: V\u00e9mola vs. V\u00e9gh 3''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Oktagon MMA]] on June 14, 2025, in [[Prague]], Czech Republic.\n\n===Background===\nThe event marked the promotion's second held in Fortuna Arena and first since [[2024 in Oktagon MMA#Oktagon 58: V\u00e9mola vs V\u00e9gh 2|Oktagon 58]] in June 2024.{{Cite web |last=Celuch |first=Martyna |date=2024-12-29 |title=OKTAGON MMA zapowiedzia\u0142 gal\u0119 na 27- tysi\u0119cznym stadionie \n|url=https://inthecage.pl/oktagon-mma-zapowiedzial-gale-na-27-tysiecznym-stadionie/ |access-date=2025-01-01 |website=InTheCage.pl |language=pl-PL}}\n\nA light heavyweight trilogy between former [[Oktagon MMA#Middleweight championship|Oktagon Middleweight]] and [[Oktagon MMA#Light heavyweight championship|Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Karlos V\u00e9mola]] and former [[Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Champion]] [[Attila V\u00e9gh]] headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-01-26 |title=Judgement Day: V\u00e9mola vs. V\u00e9gh 3 announced for football stadium showdown \n|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/judgement-day-vemola-vs-vegh-3-announced-for-football-stadium-showdown/ |access-date=2025-01-26 |website=FightBook MMA |language=en-US}} The pairing first met at Oktagon 15 in November 2019, where V\u00e9gh won by first round knockout. Their second meeting took place at [[2024 in Oktagon MMA#Oktagon 58: V\u00e9mola vs V\u00e9gh 2|Oktagon 58]] in March 2023, which V\u00e9mola defended the light heavyweight title by second round submission.{{Cite web |last=Staff |date=2024-06-08 |title=Oktagon 58: Vemola vs. Vegh 2 Full Results \n|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2024/06/08/oktagon-58-vemola-vs-vegh-2-full-results/ |access-date=2025-03-05 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\nThe co-main event took place an interim Oktagon Middleweight Championship bout between [[Makhmud Muradov]] and former champion Patrik Kincl.{{Cite web |date=2025-03-13 |title=Makhmud Muradov clashes with Patrik Kincl for interim middleweight belt in football stadium at OKTAGON 72 \n|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/makhmud-muradov-clashes-with-patrik-kincl-for-interim-middleweight-belt-in-football-stadium-at-oktagon-72/ |access-date=2025-03-15 |website=FightBook MMA |language=en-US}}\n\nAn interim [[Oktagon MMA#Women's bantamweight championship|Oktagon Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between undefeated contender Lucia Szabov\u00e1 and [[Lucie Pudilov\u00e1]] was scheduled to take place at this event.{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-04-17 |title=Nejlep\u0161\u00ed karta v historii? OKTAGON 72 v Edenu hl\u00e1s\u00ed dal\u0161\u00ed titulovou bitvu \n|url=https://mmashorties.cz/nejlepsi-karta-v-historii-oktagon-72-v-edenu-hlasi-dalsi-titulovou-bitvu/ |access-date=2025-04-17 |website=MMA Shorties |language=cs}} However on May 14, it was announced that Pudilov\u00e1 withdrew from the bout due to injury.{{Cite web |date=2025-05-15 |title=Nejv\u011bt\u0161\u00ed \u017eensk\u00fd z\u00e1pas v historii Oktagonu znovu pad\u00e1. Szabov\u00e1 si na Pudilovou bude muset po\u010dkat \n|url=https://www.mmamag.cz/lucie-pudilova-lucia-szabova-oktagon-mma-zraneni/ |access-date=2025-05-19 |website=MMAmag.cz |language=cs}}\n\nCurrent Oktagon Middleweight Champion Kerim Engizek and Mick Stanton were scheduled to meet in a quarterfinal of [[#Oktagon Middleweight Tournament|Oktagon Middleweight Tournament]], but Engizek withdrew from the event due to injury and the pairing would be postponed to a future date.{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-06-08 |title=Bohu\u017eel je to tak! Turnaj OKTAGON 72 hl\u00e1s\u00ed nep\u0159\u00edjemnou ztr\u00e1tu \n|url=https://mmashorties.cz/bohuzel-je-to-tak-turnaj-oktagon-72-hlasi-neprijemnou-ztratu/ |access-date=2025-06-08 |website=MMA Shorties |language=cs}}\n\nOn June 9, the middleweight tournament quarterfinal between Mat\u011bj Pe\u0148\u00e1z and former interim middleweight champion Piotr Wawrzyniak was removed from the event due to Pe\u0148\u00e1z has suffered an infection.{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-06-09 |title=Mat\u011bj Pe\u0148\u00e1z vysv\u011btluje odstoupen\u00ed z Edenu a omlouv\u00e1 se fanou\u0161k\u016fm \n|url=https://mmashorties.cz/matej-penaz-vysvetluje-odstoupeni-z-edenu-a-omlouva-se-fanouskum/ |access-date=2025-06-11 |website=MMA Shorties |language=cs}} Subsequently, Frederic Vosgr\u00f6ne withdrew from the bout against Lucas Alsina due to suffered an infection and was replaced by [[Daniel \u0160kvor]].{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-06-10 |title=Tak to klobouk dol\u016f! Republic\n|attendance=\n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=\n}}\n\n'''''Oktagon 74: Bolander vs. Szabov\u00e1''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by [[Oktagon MMA]] on August 9, 2025, in [[Prague]], Czech Republic.\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by an [[Oktagon MMA#Women's bantamweight championship|Oktagon Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion Cecilie Bolander and undefeated contender Lucia Szabov\u00e1.{{Cite web |date=2025-06-14 |title=OKTAGON 74: Summer MMA Spectacle Returns to \u0160tvanice with Bantamweight Title Fight and \u20ac1M Quarterfinal Showdowns \n|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/oktagon-74-summer-mma-spectacle-returns-to-stvanice-with-bantamweight-title-fight-and-e1m-quarterfinal-showdowns/ |access-date=2025-06-15 |website=FightBook MMA |language=en-US}}\n\nAn [[Oktagon MMA#Bantamweight championship|Oktagon Bantamweight Championship]] bout between [[Igor Severino]] and former [[Cage Warriors#Cage Warriors Engizek vs. Humburger ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Oktagon 77: Engizek vs. Humburger\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Oktagon MMA]]\n|date=October 4, 2025\n|venue=[[Tipos ar\u00e9na|Ondrej Nepela on October 4, 2025, in [[Bratislava]], Slovakia.\n\n===Background===\nAn [[Oktagon MMA#Heavyweight championship|Oktagon Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion (also a current [[Oktagon MMA#Light heavyweight championship| Oktagon Light Heavyweight Champion]]) [[Will Fleury]] and former champion [[Martin Buday]] was rumoured to headline the event, but it never materialized due to Fleury had been announced in advance that his cousin would be holding a wedding ceremony that day.{{Cite web |date=2025-08-11 |title=UFC Veteran Martin Buday To Face Champ Will Fleury in Return [[Munich]], Germany.\n\n===Background===\nAn [[Oktagon MMA#Lightweight championship|Oktagon Lightweight Championship]] bout for the vacant title between former champion [[Mateusz Legierski]] and Attila Korkmaz headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-10-09 |title=Oktagon ozn\u00e1mil ve\u013ek\u00fd titulov\u00fd z\u00e1pas vo v\u00e1he, ktor\u00e1 aktu\u00e1lne nem\u00e1 svojho |language=sk}}\n\nThe main event was originally scheduled as a welterweight bout between Christian Jungwirth and UFC veteran [[Niklas Stolze]], but the bout was shifted to the co-main event instead and remained five rounds.{{Cite web |last=Bartos |first=Pavel |date=2025-09-13 |title=Tak to bude hukot! Czech Republic.\n\n===Background===\nAn [[Oktagon MMA#Heavyweight championship|Oktagon Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion (also a current [[Oktagon MMA#Light heavyweight championship| Oktagon Light Heavyweight Champion]]) [[Will Fleury]] and former champion [[Martin Buday]] headlined the event.{{Cite web |last=Staff |date=2025-10-24 |title=Will Fleury vs. Martin Buday Heavyweight Title Fight Set for Oktagon 81 |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/10/24/will-fleury-vs-martin-buday-heavyweight-title-fight-set-for-oktagon-81/ |access-date=2025-10-25 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\nAn [[Oktagon MMA#Flyweight Championship|Oktagon Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Zhalgas Zhumagulov]] and [[David Dvo\u0159\u00e1k]] served as the co-main event.{{Cite web |date=2025-10-14 |title=Oktagon ozn\u00e1mil ve\u013ek\u00fd titulov\u00fd z\u00e1pas vo v\u00e1he, ktor\u00e1 aktu\u00e1lne nem\u00e1 svojho \u0161ampi\u00f3na! \n|url=https://sport.aktuality.sk/mma-fanatik/oktagon-oznamil-velky-titulovy-zapas-vo-vahe-ktora-aktualne-nema-svojho-sampiona/ |access-date=2025-10-15 |website=sport.aktuality.sk |language=sk}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[Oktagon MMA#Welterweight championship|Oktagon Welterweight Champion]] Kaik Brito and Amiran Gogoladze was scheduled for this event. However, Gogoladze withdrew from the event due to a knee injury.{{Cite web |date=2025-12-10 |title=Tak to je poriadna smola. ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Oktagon 82: Engizek vs. Jotko\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Oktagon MMA]]\n|date=January 17, 2026\n|venue=[[PSD Bank Dome]]\n|city=[[D\u00fcsseldorf]], Germany [[D\u00fcsseldorf]], Germany.\n\n===Background===\nIn the final of [[2025 in Oktagon MMA#Oktagon Middleweight Tournament|Oktagon Middleweight Tournament]] bout between the current [[Oktagon MMA#Middleweight championship|Oktagon Middleweight Champion]] Kerim Engizek and [[Krzysztof Jotko]] headlined the event.{{Cite web |date=2025-10-15 |title=Pozn\u00e1me miesto a d\u00e1tum ve\u013ek\u00e9ho fin\u00e1le Tipsport Gamechanger v strednej v\u00e1he, v ktorom nast\u00fapia Engizek a Jotko! \n|url=https://sport.aktuality.sk/mma-fanatik/pozname-miesto-a-datum-velkeho-finale-tipsport-gamechanger-v-strednej-vahe-v-ktorom-nastupia-engizek-a-jotko/ |access-date=2025-11-15 |website=sport.aktuality.sk |language=sk}}\n\nA middleweight bout between Dominik Humburger and Jamie Cordero was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web |date=2025-10-30 |title=Dominik Humburger takmer porazil \u0161ampi\u00f3na Engizeka. Teraz mu organiz\u00e1cia ozn\u00e1mila \u010fal\u0161\u00ed skvel\u00fd z\u00e1pas \n|url=https://sport.aktuality.sk/mma-fanatik/dominik-humburger-takmer-porazil-sampiona-engizeka-teraz-mu-organizacia-oznamila-dalsi-skvely-zapas/ |access-date=2025-11-27 |website=sport.aktuality.sk |language=sk}} However, Cordero withdrew from the bout due to an injury and was replaced by [[David Zawada]].{{Cite web |date=2025-11-26 |title=Dominik Humburger m\u00e1 potvrden\u00fd nov\u00fd z\u00e1pas! [[Szczecin]], Poland.\n\n===Background===\n[[File:1506 Arena Szczecin ZS.jpg|thumb|left|The [[Enea Arena]] hosted the promotion's debut in Poland, which became the 5th country to hold a Oktagon event.{{Cite web |last=Celuch |first=Martyna |date=2025-11-01 |title=Materla wyst\u0105pi na pierwszej gali Oktagon MMA w Polsce. Poznali\u015bmy termin i dat\u0119 |url=https://inthecage.pl/materla-wystapi-na-pierwszej-gali-oktagon-mma-w-polsce-poznalismy-termin-i-date/ |access-date=2025-11-02 |website=InTheCage.pl |language=pl-PL}}]]\n\nA [[Oktagon MMA#Lightweight championship|Oktagon Lightweight Championship]] bout between current two-time champion [[Mateusz Legierski]] and G\u00f6khan Aksu was scheduled to headline this event.{{Cite web |date=2025-12-12 |title=Oktagon 86 in Poland Features Mateusz Legierski Title Defense Against G\u00f6khan Aksu |url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/oktagon-86-in-poland-features-mateusz-legierski-title-defense-against-gokhan-aksu/ |access-date=2025-12-12 |website=www.lowkickmma.com |language=en-US}} However, Legierski announced that he has broken his thumb and not competing at this event.{{Cite web |last=Zubkiewicz |first=Bart\u0142omiej |date=2026-03-03 |title=Mateusz Legierski nie zawalczy na gali Oktagon 86 w Szczecinie |url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/mateusz-legierski/mateusz-legierski-nie-zawalczy-na-gali-oktagon-86-w-szczecinie |access-date=2026-03-04 |website=MMA Rocks! |language=pl-PL}}\n\nAs a results, a catchweight bout of 196 pounds between former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Middleweight championship|KSW Middleweight Champion]] [[Micha\u0142 Materla]] and Christian Jungwirth served as the new main event, which the bout contested under \"Stand and Bang\" rules with five three-minute rounds.{{Cite web |last=Chmura |first=Tomasz |date=2026-01-31 |title=Micha\u0142 Materla zmierzy si\u0119 z Christianem Jungwirthem w Szczecinie na Oktagon 86 |url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/michal-materla/michal-materla-zmierzy-sie-z-christianem-jungwirthem-w-szczecinie-na-oktagon-86 |access-date=2026-03-04 |website=MMA Rocks! |language=pl-PL}}{{Cite web |last=Wojew\u00f3dka |first=Oskar |date=2026-03-03 |title= Kontuzja Polaka przed wa\u017cn\u0105 walk\u0105. martial arts]] event held by [[Oktagon MMA]] on June 6, 2026, in [[Bratislava]], Slovakia.\n\n===Background===\nThe reigning [[Oktagon MMA#Bantamweight championship|Oktagon Bantamweight Champion]] [[Igor Severino]] put his bantamweight title on the line against the reigning [[Oktagon MMA#Flyweight championship|Oktagon Flyweight Champion]] [[Zhalgas Zhumagulov]] at the main event. Severino and Zhumagulov has been a record unbeaten since his UFC release.{{Cite web |last=King |first=Nolan |title=OKTAGON books champ vs. champ superfight between UFC alumni |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/ufc/2026/04/06/oktagon-igor-severino-vs-zhalgas-zhumagulov-ufc/89489262007/ |access-date=2026-04-06 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA 194-pounds catchweight bout between Marek Maz\u00fach and David Ho\u0161ek was scheduled at this event. Engizek vs. Jotko 2 ==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Oktagon 91: Engizek vs. Jotko 2\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Oktagon MMA]]\n|date=July 11, 2026\n|venue=[[Lanxess Arena]]\n|city=[[Cologne]], Germany.\n\n===Background===\nA [[Oktagon MMA#Middleweight championship|Oktagon Middleweight Championship]] bout between current champion Kerim Engizek and [[2025 in Oktagon MMA#Oktagon Middleweight Tournament|Oktagon Middleweight Tournament]] winner [[Krzysztof Jotko]] headlined the event.{{Cite web |last=Madej |first=Jakub |date=2026-03-08 |title=Krzysztof Jotko vs Kerim Engizek o pas mistrzowski Oktagon MMA w kategorii \u015bredniej! |url=https://www.mmarocks.pl/oktagon-91/krzysztof-jotko-vs-kerim-engizek-o-pas-mistrzowski-oktagon-mma-w-kategorii-sredniej |access-date=2026-03-09 |website=MMA Rocks! |language=pl-PL}} The pairing previously fought at [[Oktagon 82]] in January, which Jotko won the tournament by first round submission.{{Cite web |date=2026-01-17 |title=Krzysztof
